Kết quả chấm bài

The student's report covers keylogger behavior with ADS stealth mode, including process execution, file system operations, and network activity. However, it misses persistence details, has contradictions in registry operations, and includes noise in network analysis. Evidence traceability is partial, and some findings are unsupported.
